Method: 采用几何热力学（GTD）框架，计算平衡流形的标量曲率$\mathcal{R}$；引入无量纲临界振幅比$Q^i_{j}$作为普适分类参数；运用贝叶斯推断和马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法统计重建零曲率曲线并验证一致性。

Result: 曲率奇点精确再现了宏观临界现象；零曲率$\mathcal{R}=0$标识吸引/排斥力平衡态；临界振幅比$Q^i_{j}$为不变量，按临界强度组织分子物种并编码普适标度特征；MCMC后验分布支持GTD几何标度行为的一致性。

Conclusion: GTD流形编码了底层热力学模型的非平凡信息，零曲率曲线和临界振幅比可作为分子间相互作用的有效分类工具，为实际流体的统一热力学描述提供了几何化途径。

Abstract: We introduce a unified entropic framework for real fluids that encompasses the van der Waals, Berthelot, Redlich Kwong, and Peng Robinson equations of state within a common thermodynamic description. The corresponding microscopic interactions are then explored using Geometrothermodynamics, GTD, through the scalar curvature $mathcal{R}$ of the equilibrium manifold. We show that curvature singularities accurately reproduce macroscopic critical phenomena, while vanishing curvature $\mathcal{R}=0$ identifies specific thermodynamic states where attractive and repulsive intermolecular forces effectively balance. Furthermore, we introduce a set of dimensionless critical-amplitude ratios $Q^i_{j}$, which reveal universal geometric features of the critical regime. Although individual critical amplitudes exhibit a logarithmic dependence on the system size, these invariant ratios organize different molecular species according to the strength of criticality and encode universal scaling features, suggesting their potential as robust classification parameters. Finally, employing Bayesian inference and Markov Chain Monte Carlo, MCMC methods, we statistically reconstruct the zero-curvature curves. The posterior distributions support the consistency of the geometric scaling behavior, demonstrating that the GTD manifold encodes non-trivial information about the underlying thermodynamical models.

</details>


### [86] [Revisiting the non-equilibrium phase transitions of the continuous-trait Axelrod model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10381)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将方法论焦点转向中位数̃μ的标度分析以及完整概率分布P(μ)的考察，揭示了独立模拟运行中具有不相交峰值的清晰双峰结构。

Result: F=2时系统在当代意义上呈现真正的混合相变：存在微小但有限的潜跳变（μ_c≈0.089），临界阈值d_c≈0.0784，从下方以平均场指数β≈1/2的非解析幂律标度趋近临界点。F=3时由于更高特征空间维度抑制局域涨落，呈现传统非混合的一级相变。

Conclusion: 该框架成功应用于离散Poisson变体模型，确认了F=2时连续相变和F=3时不连续非混合相变的已知结果，从而建立了Axelrod类系统相变的统一表征。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium phase transitions of the continuous-trait Axelrod model, an agent-based framework where individual culture is represented by a vector of $F$ continuous features confined to the interval $(0,1)$. Local interactions are governed by a metric similarity threshold $d$, which acts as a continuous control parameter of social tolerance. The dynamics inevitably freeze into one of two absorbing configuration classes: an ordered, homogeneous monocultural state at high tolerance, or a highly fragmented, disordered state at low tolerance. While previous studies characterized the transition as hybrid based on the continuous behavior of the domain density $μ$ alongside a discontinuous jump in the largest domain fraction $ρ$, we show that this apparent continuity is an artifact of severe finite-size masking effects. By shifting the methodological focus to the scaling of the median $\tildeμ$ and analyzing the full probability distributions $P(μ)$, we unveil a clear bimodal structure with disjoint maxima across independent simulation runs. Our results reveal that for $F=2$, the system undergoes a genuinely hybrid transition in the contemporary sense, featuring a tiny but finite latent jump ($μ_c \approx 0.089$) at the critical threshold $d_c \approx 0.0784$ while scaling toward it from below via a non-analytical power law with a mean-field exponent $β\approx 1/2$. Conversely, for $F=3$, the higher trait-space dimensionality suppresses local fluctuations, yielding a traditional, non-hybrid first-order transition. We apply this framework to the alternative discrete Poisson variant of the model, successfully confirming its known continuous transition for $F=2$ and discontinuous, non-hybrid transition for $F=3$, thereby establishing a unified characterization of phase transitions in Axelrod-like systems.

</details>

*Masato Hisakado*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Wigner型随机矩阵中行-列共享随机因子产生的非对角相关性如何影响谱行为，特别是离群特征值的出现机制及其相变行为。

Method: 采用Karhunen-Loève展开将相关矩阵的离群特征值映射到Brownian运动的累积和积分算子；通过紧Volterra积分算子谱分析得到奇异值封闭解；利用BBP相变理论分析耦合强度增加时的特征值脱谱行为。

Conclusion: 这种机制可推广到更广泛的相关结构族，每种情况下的临界层次均由关联紧积分算子的谱决定。

Abstract: We study a Wigner-type random matrix in which the off-diagonal correlation between entries is generated by a random factor shared among all entries in a given row and column, with the coupling strength held fixed as the matrix size grows. Although the bulk spectral moments remain those of the pure semicircle law, we show that the underlying correlation matrix decomposes into a vanishing bulk together with a countable family of outlier eigenvalues that, at fixed rank $k$, converge to the singular values of a compact Volterra (cumulative-sum) integral operator -- obtained in closed form via the classical Karhunen--Loève expansion of Brownian motion and confirmed numerically to better than one percent across the top twenty such values. Each singular value drives an independent Baik--Ben Arous--Péché (BBP) transition as the coupling strength increases, producing an evenly spaced, discrete hierarchy of critical points -- rather than a single transition -- at each of which one further eigenvalue detaches from the semicircle edge, in close agreement with direct diagonalization. We show that this mechanism generalizes to a broader family of correlation structures, with the critical hierarchy in every case set by the spectrum of an associated compact integral operator.

</details>

*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双轴插值框架，将空间维度D和自旋分量数n视为连续参数，通过锚定精确解（如Onsager解、平均场理论、球模型极限）来预测O(n)普适类的临界指数和耦合常数。在D轴上，3D Ising模型的临界耦合和临界指数与基准值吻合良好；在n轴上，通过分析单调性条件，成功预测了O(3)（Heisenberg模型）的临界指数，并扩展至非整数自旋O(2.5)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的O(n)普适类研究通常分别处理维度D和自旋分量数n，缺乏统一的解析框架。作者希望建立一个双轴插值方法，将D和n作为连续参数，连接已知的精确解（如D=2 Onsager解、D→∞平均场、n→∞球模型极限），从而预测中间值（如D=3、n=3）的临界性质，并阐明插值成功的条件。

Method: (1) D轴插值：以D=2的Onsager解和D→∞的平均场理论为锚点，构造闭合形式的插值函数预测3D Ising临界耦合；结合Wilson-Fisher固定点约束进行多项式插值得到临界指数。(2) n轴插值：提出单调性相容性准则——仅当可观测量在锚点间单调变化时双锚点插值才有效；对于违反单调性的临界耦合K_c(n)，改用1/n^2微扰展开；对于满足单调性的临界指数ν(n)，采用插值方法。

Result: D轴：3D Ising临界耦合K_c=0.2204（基准0.22165），临界指数ν=2/3≈0.6667（基准0.6299），β=31/96≈0.3229（基准0.3265），η=35/864≈0.0405（基准0.0362），在3≤D<4范围内重现共形自举结果。n轴：ν(3)=0.7493（基准0.7112），β(3)=0.3797（基准0.3689），γ(3)=1.489（基准1.396），以及非整数自旋预测ν(2.5)=0.7143。

Conclusion: 双轴插值框架是一种统一且具有预测性的临界现象分析方法，能够在不引入可调参数的情况下从精确解出发预测三维临界性质。同时，研究揭示了插值成功的结构条件（单调性准则），为未来在其他普适类中应用该框架提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We develop a two-axis interpolation framework for the O$(n)$ universality family, treating the spatial dimension $D$ and the spin-component number $n$ as independent continuous parameters connecting exact limiting solutions. On the spatial axis, anchoring between the Onsager solution at $D=2$ and mean-field theory at $D\to\infty$ yields a closed-form prediction for the 3D Ising critical coupling that agrees well with Monte Carlo benchmarks $K_c = 0.2204$ (benchmark: $0.22165$) with no adjustable parameters. Wilson--Fisher-constrained polynomial interpolation gives $ν=2/3$, $β=31/96$, and $η=35/864$ at $D=3$ (benchmarks: $0.6299$, $0.3265$, $0.0362$), and reproduces conformal-bootstrap results across $3 \le D < 4$. On the spin axis, we establish a necessary compatibility criterion: two-anchor interpolation succeeds only for observables that vary monotonically between the anchor values. The critical coupling $K_c(n)$ violates this criterion because the Heisenberg value falls below the spherical limit, whereas the correlation-length exponent $ν(n)$ satisfies it. A perturbative $1/n^2$ expansion yields $ν(3) = 0.7493$ (benchmark: $0.7112$), and propagation through exact scaling relations gives $β(3) = 0.3797$ (benchmark: $0.3689$) and $γ(3) = 1.489$ (benchmark: $1.396$), without introducing additional parameters. The framework naturally extends to non-integer spin, producing the prediction $ν(2.5) = 0.7143$ for the O$(2.5)$ universality class. These results establish dimensional and spin interpolation as a unified and predictive approach to critical phenomena, while clarifying the structural conditions under which interpolation succeeds.

</details>


### [89] [Finite-time Scaling of the surface special transition in a 3D classical Heisenberg model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11066)
*Dongxu Liu,Zhe Wang,Shuai Yin,Zheng Yan*

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究了三维经典海森堡模型在开放边界条件下跨越特殊表面相变的非平衡驱动动力学，发现了从异常对数临界态驱动时，标度关系会出现对数修正，形式为 $M^{2}_{s}\propto R^{(1+η_{s})/r_{s}}[\log(LR^{1/η_{s}})]^{-q}$，并验证了该形式可由有限时间标度理论推广得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三维经典海森堡模型在开放边界条件下，跨越特殊表面相变的非平衡驱动动力学，特别是从异常对数边界临界态（具有对数而非幂律衰减关联）驱动时，标度行为是否出现对数修正。

Conclusion: 异常对数初始态会改变非平衡临界标度行为，将边界FTS推广到超越常规幂律初始条件的范围；该修正形式可通过在FTS框架中纳入对数初始态记忆得到，并在多种系统尺寸和驱动速率下实现良好的数据塌缩。

Abstract: We investigate nonequilibrium driven dynamics across the special surface phase transition in the three-dimensional classical Heisenberg model with open boundaries, where tuning the surface coupling gives access to an extraordinary-log boundary critical state characterized by logarithmic, rather than power-law, decay of correlations. Using Monte Carlo simulations, we realize four driving protocols: temperature heating and cooling across the special transition, and surface-coupling ramps from the ordinary and extraordinary-log critical states into the special point. For temperature-driven protocols, the surface order parameter obeys a generalization of the finite-time scaling (FTS) and the Kibble-Zurek mechanism. The central finding emerges when the system is driven from the extraordinary-log critical state: the large-rate scaling relation acquires a logarithmic correction and takes the novel form $M^{2}_{s}\propto R^{(1+η_{s})/r_{s}}[\log(LR^{1/η_{s}})]^{-q}$ , where $R$ is the driving rate, $L$ the system size, $η_{s}$ the surface anomalous dimension, $r_{s}$ the scaling dimension of $R$, and $q$ the exponent governing the logarithmic boundary criticality. We demonstrate that this form follows from the general FTS framework by incorporating the logarithmic initial-state memory, and we achieve excellent data collapse over a wide range of system sizes and driving rates. Our results establish that extraordinary-log initial states alter nonequilibrium critical scaling, extending boundary FTS beyond conventional power-law initial conditions.

These scaling relations are used to produce precise estimates for the autocorrelation exponent $λ$ and dynamical exponent $z$ with deliberately small lattices. Abstract: We investigate the ground-state and finite-temperature properties of a mixed spin-1/2 and spin-1 Ising model on a decorated square (Lieb) lattice incorporating a uniaxial single-ion anisotropy and magnetic field. By employing the generalized decoration-iteration transformation, the model is mapped exactly onto an effective spin-1/2 Ising model on the square lattice characterized by an effective nearest-neighbor interaction and an effective field. The studied model consequently becomes exactly solvable even for finite values of the applied magnetic field whenever the effective field vanishes. The ground-state analysis reveals three distinct phases: ferrimagnetic phase (FRI), disordered phase (DP), and ferromagnetic (FM) phase. The ground-state boundary between FRI and DP phases gives rise to a dome-shaped surface of discontinuous thermal phase transitions, which is terminated by a line of Ising-type critical points associated with continuous thermal phase transitions. Both continuous and discontinuous thermal phase transitions belong to the exactly solvable parameter regime defined by a vanishing effective field in spite of the fact that the applied magnetic field is finite. Abstract: We exactly investigate the ground-state, magnetic, and thermodynamic properties of a spin-1 Blume-Capel diamond chain in a magnetic field by means of the transfer-matrix method. After establishing the complete ground-state phase diagram and characterizing each ground-state spin configuration, we examine the finite-temperature behavior in the vicinity of selected phase boundaries and triple points. It is demonstrated that an extremely small energy gap between a nondegenerate ground state and competing macroscopically degenerate low-lying excited states gives rise to entropically-driven pseudo-transitions. These pseudo-transitions manifest themselves through abrupt but continuous changes in the magnetization and entropy resembling discontinuous jumps, while the magnetic susceptibility and specific heat exhibit exceptionally sharp yet finite peaks resembling divergences. Our results provide the exact evidence that pseudo-transitions can also emerge in the classical spin-1 Blume-Capel model and thereby extend the class of one-dimensional spin systems known to display pseudo-transitions.

Abstract: We develop a Fokker-Planck description of the dynamics of wealth distribution in a stochastic multiplicative economic growth model with taxation and redistribution, as introduced by P.M.C. de Oliveira. Extending the original formulation, our theoretical framework includes general redistribution protocols, encompassing a broad class of state-dependent transfer mechanisms. As a particular case, we investigate a two-state protocol designed to emulate conditional cash transfer programs. Analytical expressions for the stationary wealth distributions are derived, revealing how the interplay between multiplicative noise, taxation, and redistribution shapes the emergence of inequality. The theoretical results are corroborated by agent-based simulations. To quantify and compare the impact of the different protocols, we employ the Gini index as a measure of inequality. Our analysis highlights how specific nonuniform redistribution schemes can significantly mitigate wealth disparities.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Mpemba效应在惯性不可忽略的欠阻尼体系（underdamped regime）中是否成立，即将该效应的研究从经典的过阻尼系统推广到更一般的欠阻尼动力学系统。

Method: 采用微扰理论（perturbative）分析欠阻尼布朗粒子在势阱中的动力学行为，分别研究有限阻尼和超弱阻尼两种极限情况，并通过双阱势数值模拟验证理论结果。

Result: 1) 若过阻尼极限下存在Mpemba效应，则在足够大但有限的阻尼下该效应持续存在；2) 在超弱阻尼极限下，光滑单阱势阱中Mpemba效应无法发生；3) 在更复杂的势阱（如双阱势）中，超弱阻尼极限下Mpemba效应可以出现。

Conclusion: Mpemba效应在欠阻尼体系中的存在性依赖于阻尼大小和势阱的复杂程度：有限阻尼下可继承过阻尼极限的性质，而超弱阻尼下需要复杂势阱才能实现该效应。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect is the counterintuitive phenomenon in which an initially hotter system cools faster than a colder, otherwise identical system. It has been experimentally demonstrated in various classical overdamped systems. Here, we explore the existence of the same effect in a regime where inertia cannot be neglected, namely, the underdamped regime. We consider the underdamped dynamics of a Brownian particle in a potential. We show perturbatively that, if the effect exists in the overdamped limit, it persists for sufficiently large but finite damping. In the ultra-weak-damping limit, we show that the effect cannot occur for smooth confining single-well potentials with canonical initial states, but can arise in more complex potentials. We demonstrate our results numerically using double-well potentials, the canonical setting for the Mpemba effect in the overdamped limit.

</details>


### [95] [Slow is fast: raising barriers to accelerate thermal relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11877)
*Yubo Wang,Zhiyue Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 对于可逆系统弛豫到平衡态，直觉上最快策略是降低所有能垒（打开所有门）。本文发现该直觉在三个层面成立，但存在第四个反直觉的最优层面——特征向量旋转，通过非交换性实现更快弛豫。最优调度为bang-bang控制，示例中相比全开策略终端残差降低130倍。同时证明非交换性是必要条件，并给出反向延迟问题的对偶调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究可逆系统弛豫过程中，是否可以通过超越直觉的“全开能垒”策略实现更快的有限时间弛豫，揭示非交换性在弛豫动力学中的关键作用。

Method: 理论分析四个层面（局部电导、f-散度瞬时速度、弛豫本征值、本征向量旋转），提出bang-bang最优控制调度，建立非交换性必要条件定理，并给出反向延迟问题的对偶调度。

Result: 全开能垒策略在前三个层面最优，但第四层面（本征向量旋转）存在反直觉的更优调度。示例中最佳调度相比全开策略终端残差降低130倍，相比最优静态调控降低7倍。非交换性是关键，对偶调度在延迟问题中比全闭策略更有效。

Conclusion: 能垒控制通过重排固定耗散预算的时序实现加速或延迟弛豫，而不对约化马尔可夫系统做功。非交换性为超越静态直觉调控提供了必要机制。

Abstract: For a reversible system relaxing to equilibrium, the obvious fastest strategy is to lower all kinetic barriers (open all gates). We find that such intuition holds at three levels: the all-open-gate strategy achieves the highest local conductance, it maximizes the instantaneous speed of approach in every $f$-divergence, and it simultaneously maximizes all relaxation eigenvalues. Nevertheless, we show that a counter-intuitive finite-time optimum lies beyond this intuition and operates at a fourth level, invisible to all three: eigenvector rotation. Noncommutativity enables timed schedules to reproject residual amplitudes across relaxation modes, thereby achieving faster relaxation. Optimal schedules are bang--bang. In our illustrative example, the best-found schedule also employs counter-gating, transiently raising selected barriers, and reduces the terminal residual by a factor of $130$ relative to all-open, and by $7$ relative to the best static landscape. A no-go theorem shows that noncommutativity is necessary: commuting generators collapse every schedule to a static time-averaged landscape, worse than the intuitive static control. In the reverse problem, the dual schedule preserves nonequilibrium free energy far more effectively than intuitively keeping all barriers at maximum heights. Whether accelerating or delaying relaxation, barrier control performs no work on the reduced Markov system; it only re-times a fixed total dissipation budget.

</details>

Method: 采用超相对论性展开（w→∞）对NS-NS和杂化超引力场进行展开，通过适当的膨胀子标度消除发散；利用场重定义简化GS机制；从变分原理和相对论方程展开两个途径推导卡罗尔运动方程。

Abstract: We construct the Carrollian limit of NS--NS and heterotic supergravity through an ultra-relativistic expansion of the fields. An appropriate scaling of the dilaton renders the measure finite and compensates the divergences arising from the NS-NS supergravity Lagrangian, giving a finite action as $w\rightarrow\infty$. We then extend the construction to heterotic supergravity (neglecting fermions) by incorporating the non-Abelian gauge field together with the Green--Schwarz (GS) mechanism. The resulting theory contains a finite gauge sector consistently coupled to gravity, and the GS mechanism for the Carrollian 1-form field can be trivialized imposing field redefinitions. Then, we investigate the Carrollian equations of motion by both expanding the relativistic equations and deriving them from a variational principle. We also show that the leading $α'$-corrected $\hat{\rm Riem}^2$ contribution remains finite under a rescaling of the string parameter $α'\rightarrow \frac{α'_c}{w^2}$, opening further research towards the full four-derivative effective action. Finally, we discuss the potential connection with the worldsheet formalism of the Carrollian string theory.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a microscopic quantum membrane paradigm from the matrix quantum mechanics of black holes [1]. It was proposed that a quantum black hole is described by a fuzzy sphere together with a half-filled Fermi sea of horizon partons. We show that the topology of the fuzzy sphere induces a Berry monopole, providing a microscopic origin for the monopole appearing in the tunneling description of the decay of quantum black hole. Because the partons couple to the fuzzy sphere as fundamental degrees of freedom, they are sensitive to this monopole and form Lowest-Landau-Level (LLL) states rather than ordinary propagating two-dimensional fermions. Their guiding-center dynamics generates Ohmic, Hall, and polarization currents on the horizon. To couple these currents to an external electromagnetic field, we introduce a two-block matrix configuration comprising black-hole, environmental, and bifundamental link sectors. Off-diagonal link modes become tachyonic near the fuzzy sphere and condense, dynamically locking the horizon gauge field to the boundary value of the external Maxwell field. The parton current thereby becomes a physical boundary source for the exterior field. Our construction replaces the fictitious membrane of the classical paradigm with a dynamical quantum membrane populated by microscopic LLL degrees of freedom. The resulting boundary condition generalizes the classical ingoing membrane condition and yields explicit quantum, frequency-dependent, and helicity-dependent corrections, offering a possible probe of the microscopic quantum structure of the horizon.

</details>


### [98] [Anomaly cancellation for two $U(1)$ factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09879)
*Ben Gripaios,Khoi Le Nguyen Nguyen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于具有U(1)⁺K abelian部分的4维规范理论，局部反常消去条件等价于在有理数上寻找三次超曲面的(K-1)维射影子空间这一代数/算术几何问题。以U(1)²和6个费米子为例，该问题对应于Segre三次原像三折叠中的线簇Fano簇，其包含15个平面分量（非手征费米子）和6个分裂del Pezzo 5次曲面分量（手征费米子），所有分量均为有理簇，可完全参数化并描述其拓扑与渐近分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将4维规范理论中abelian部分的反常年消去条件转化为代数/算术几何中寻找三次超曲面上射影子空间的问题，从而利用几何方法系统求解反常消去条件。

Method: 建立反常消去条件与三次超曲面射影子空间之间的等价关系，然后针对秩为2的具体实例（如U(1)²+6费米子）进行几何分析，识别对应的Fano簇及其不可约分支结构。

Result: 发现U(1)²与6费米子的最简单非平凡实例对应于Segre三次原像三折叠的线簇Fano簇，该簇由15个平面分量（非手征费米子）和6个分裂del Pezzo 5次曲面分量（手征费米子）组成，所有分量均为有理簇，可实现完全参数化和渐近分布描述。

Conclusion: 反常消去条件与代数几何中三次超曲面射影子空间问题等价，利用这一几何重构可系统求解多种秩2实例，并揭示其丰富的几何结构。

Abstract: We show that solving the abelian part of the local anomaly cancellation conditions for a 4-d gauge theory whose gauge Lie algebra has an abelian summand with rank $K \geq 1$ is equivalent to the problem in algebraic/arithmetic geometry of finding $(K-1)$-dimensional projective linear subspaces of a cubic hypersurface over the rational numbers, where the cubic is determined by the data of the semisimple summand and the representation thereof carried by the Weyl fermions. We then use this reformulation to solve a variety of examples with rank 2.
  The simplest non-trivial example from physics, namely gauge Lie group $U(1)^2$ and six fermions, nevertheless has a rich (and well-studied) geometry: it corresponds to the Fano variety of lines in the Segre cubic primal threefold. This is a surface with 15 irreducible components that are planes (which correspond to lines lying in the 15 planes in the Segre cubic primal and which give rise to non-chiral fermions) and 6 components that are split del Pezzo surfaces of degree 5 (which give rise to chiral fermions). These components are all rational varieties, enabling all solutions to the anomaly cancellation conditions to be parametrized and their collective properties (e.g. their topology and asymptotic distribution) to be described.

</details>

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过玻色化方法在二维修正Villain标量模型中构建了手征格点费米子算符，证明了其无倍增器但非局域性，且非局域性不妨碍超局域模型的非反常对称性规范耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用玻色化实现手征费米子的格点正则化，避免传统格点费米子方案中的倍增器问题，同时检验Nielsen-Ninomiya定理的限制。

Method: 在二维修正Villain标量模型中构造超局域作用量和超局域对称性，通过玻色化映射重建格点狄拉克算符，计算其关联函数。

Result: 重建的格点狄拉克算符无倍增器，但呈现非局域性，与Nielsen-Ninomiya定理一致。

Conclusion: 非局域性在有限格距下不阻碍对模型非反常对称性进行规范耦合，整体模型仍保持超局域性。

Abstract: Thanks to bosonization, bosonic lattice models can offer a lattice regularization of chiral fermions. We construct chiral lattice fermion operators in the 2D modified Villain scalar model and evaluate their correlation functions. This microscopic bosonic model has an ultra-local action and an ultra-local symmetry that realizes the fermionic chiral symmetry under bosonization. The reconstructed lattice Dirac operator has no doublers, but is consistent with the Nielsen-Ninomiya theorem because it turns out to be non-local. The non-locality of this derived quantity at finite lattice spacing does not pose any obstructions to gauging the non-anomalous symmetries of the model, which is itself ultra-local.

</details>


### [100] [Color-factor symmetry using perturbiner methods for tree-level amplitudes of Yang-Mills theory coupled to matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10006)
*Abigail R. Chriss,Mia Karlsson,Stephen G. Naculich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过递归微扰方法，对所有包含任意数量自旋0或自旋1/2物质场及至少一个胶子的树级振幅的色彩因子对称性，给出了一个简洁而通用的证明，该对称性是BCJ关系的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCJ关系是树级振幅中不依赖于螺旋度的约束，源于色-运动学对偶性。此前虽有推导，但缺乏一个针对包含物质场的一般情况下的简洁、统一的证明。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用递归微扰方法（perturbiner methods），系统地构建并分析了包含任意数量自旋0（标量）或自旋1/2（旋量）物质场以及至少一个胶子的所有树级振幅，直接验证了色彩因子对称性。

Result: 成功证明了在包含任意数量基本表示物质场（自旋0或1/2）且至少有一个胶子的所有树级振幅中，色彩因子对称性普遍成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为BCJ关系提供了更广泛的证明基础，进一步巩固了色-运动学对偶性在包含物质场的规范理论中的应用基础，简化了相关理论证明。

Abstract: Bern-Carrasco-Johansson relations are helicity-independent constraints among the partial amplitudes of tree-level processes containing gluons and possibly also matter fields. They are a consequence of color-kinematic duality, but alternatively can be derived using the color-factor symmetry of tree-level amplitudes. We use recursive perturbiner methods to present a general, streamlined proof of the color-factor symmetry of all tree-level amplitudes containing an arbitrary number of fundamental spin-zero or spin-one-half matter fields and at least one gluon.

</details>


### [101] [Superspace invariants and 3-point correlators in 3d $\mathcal{N}=3,4$ SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10133)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维$\mathcal{N}=3$和$\mathcal{N}=4$超共形场论中，非阿贝尔$R$-对称性带来的新型反对称不变量结构未被系统研究，且这些理论中带自旋算符的三点关联函数的完整分类尚不明确。

Method: 采用辅助极化旋量形式结合超空间技术，系统构建所有可能的3点不变量结构。

Result: 给出了$\mathcal{N}=3$和$\mathcal{N}=4$超共形场论中一般及守恒带自旋超场算符的三点关联函数的结构形式。$\mathcal{N}=3$守恒算符关联函数由1个宇称偶+1个宇称奇结构固定；$\mathcal{N}=4$则由2个宇称偶+1个宇称奇结构固定。

Conclusion: 完成了三维$\mathcal{N}=3,4$超共形场论中自旋算符三点关联函数的完整分类，并揭示了镜对称性破缺与第二个宇称偶结构之间的关联。

Abstract: We use the auxiliary polarization spinor formalism together with superspace techniques to construct a complete and minimal list of 3-point invariant structures in three-dimensional superconformal field theories (SCFTs) with $\mathcal{N}=3$ and $\mathcal{N}=4$ superconformal symmetry. The existence of non-abelian $R$-symmetry for $\mathcal{N}=3,4$ gives rise to novel invariant structures built from the antisymmetric invariant tensor. These invariants are used to enumerate the structural form of spinning 3-point correlators of general as well as conserved spinning superfield operators in 3d SCFTs. For conserved operators, we find that the $\mathcal{N}=3$ correlators are fixed upto one parity-even and one parity-odd structure, while $\mathcal{N}=4$ conserved correlators admit two parity-even structures and one parity-odd structure, with the second parity-even structure associated with mirror symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [102] [D-branes in $AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$ under the non-Abelian T-duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10448)
*Ali Eghbali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过在半阿贝尔对偶上应用泊松-李T-对偶（非阿贝尔T-对偶），构建了$AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$背景的非阿贝尔对偶对。研究表明，对偶背景由度量和B场支持，在小坐标下满足单圈β函数为零方程，在大坐标下保持原解结构，并通过规范变换描述找到了七种不同的D-膜胶合矩阵情况，揭示了膜上的对称对偶作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用非阿贝尔T-对偶（泊松-李T-对偶）构建$AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$背景的精确对偶对，研究对偶时空的物理性质及D-膜特性。

Method: 采用非阿贝尔T-对偶（作为半阿贝尔对偶上的泊松-李T-对偶），利用6维李群${A}_2 \otimes {A}_2 \otimes A_2$的参数化构造原始σ-模型，通过规范变换描述得到对偶映射，分析小坐标和大坐标下的渐近行为，并基于胶合矩阵研究D-膜性质。

Result: 对偶背景由含物理奇点的度量和B场支持；小坐标下对偶度量和零场强及非平凡胀子满足单圈β函数为零方程；大坐标下$AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$解被保持；找到了七种不同的胶合矩阵情况，发现膜上存在对称对偶作用。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔T-对偶成功构建了$AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$的对偶对，对偶背景在小坐标下是共形不变的，大坐标下保持原对称性，且D-膜在该对偶下形成对称的对偶链。

Abstract: We proceed to construct a non-Abelian dual pair for the $AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$ background by applying the non-Abelian T-duality (here as Poisson-Lie T-duality on a semi-Abelian double). By using a certain parameterization of the $6$-dimensional Lie group ${A}_2 \otimes {A}_2 \otimes A_2$ we construct the original $σ$-model including the $AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$ metric in the absence of $B$-field. It is shown that the dual background constructed by means of the Poisson-Lie T-duality is supported by a $B$-field and a metric whose contains a physical singularity. By studying the behavior of the dual spacetime at small $(r , y, u)$ coordinates, we show that the dual metric with a zero field strength and a non-trivial dilaton field make up a solution for the vanishing of the one-loop beta-function equations. Furthermore, at large $(r , y, u)$, it is shown that the $AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$ solution is preserved under the non-Abelian T-duality. Finally, using the duality map obtained from the canonical transformation description of the Poisson-Lie T-duality for the gluing matrix which locally defines the properties of the D-brane, we find seven different cases of the gluing matrices for the $AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$ $σ$-model and its dual pair. In this way, it is found a symmetric duality action on the branes linking together in a duality chain.

</details>


### [103] [Krylov Complexity for Time-Dependent Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10454)
*Amin Faraji Astaneh,Parsa Kafashi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间依赖哈密顿量下态的Krylov扩展复杂性，利用Floquet理论和Magnus展开提供了分析周期驱动及非周期驱动系统复杂性增长的理论框架和实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Krylov扩展复杂性分析从定常哈密顿量推广到时间依赖哈密顿量情形，填补相关理论研究空白。

Method: 对于周期驱动系统，采用Floquet理论框架并结合Magnus展开近似Floquet哈密顿量；对于非周期驱动，除全局截断Magnus展开外，还提出分段Magnus展开方法以应对全局展开失效情形。

Result: 分段Magnus展开在全局Magnus展开失去收敛性或精度时仍能可靠计算复杂性增长。

Conclusion: 为分析一大类时间依赖量子系统的复杂性增长提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We investigate Krylov spread complexity for states evolving under time-dependent Hamiltonians. For periodically driven systems, we formulate the problem within Floquet theory and show how the Magnus expansion provides a systematic approximation when the Floquet Hamiltonian is not available in closed form. We then extend this framework beyond periodic driving and demonstrate that, in addition to the globally truncated Magnus expansion, a piecewise Magnus expansion provides a reliable method when the global expansion loses convergence or accuracy. Our results provide practical tools for analyzing complexity growth in a broad class of time-dependent quantum systems.

</details>


### [104] [Certified boundary-magic witness for state-dependent proto-area in a holographic code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10663)
*Luis Lozano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四量子比特产品-EPR全息码，发现只有物质控制的键移动变形能产生边界熵的二次逻辑态依赖。通过优化信道Choi态的相干信息和半定规划最大化纠缠保真度，物理恢复达到数值最优。稳定子Renyi魔法不能表征该效应，而投影后的边界见证在键移动变形中为正，其余为零。两边界扩展保留了简并性，但展示了分裂几何结构残差和逻辑态依赖的切割开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究四量子比特产品-EPR全息码中不同变形操作对边界熵的影响，寻找能够区分不同物理效应的边界见证，并检验稳定子Renyi魔法等度量能否表征全息码中的关键物理效应。

Method: 对四量子比特产品-EPR全息码施加七种变形（局域、二分、键稳定、键移动算子），通过精确谱分析边界熵的逻辑态依赖；优化信道Choi态的相干信息实现态无关物理恢复；利用半定规划最大化纠缠保真度；投影稳定子Renyi二次型定义边界见证。

Result: 只有物质控制的键移动变形产生边界熵的二次逻辑态依赖；物理恢复在固定耦合下达到半定规划最优值；恢复熵减法留下非零变分原面积响应；稳定子Renyi魔法对无边界响应的变形非零故不能表征该效应；投影边界见证对键移动为正其余为零；两边界扩展保留简并但呈现分裂几何结构残差和逻辑态依赖切割开关。

Conclusion: 建立了有限码边界见证，但并非普适魔法定律或连续引力动力学。证明了特定变形下的边界效应可被精确刻画，且稳定子魔法不适用于该场景。

Abstract: We study a four-qubit product-EPR holographic code whose reconstructing region contains matter and one leg of a geometry bond. Seven deformations compare local, bipartite, bond-stabilizing, and bond-moving operators. Exact spectra show that only matter-controlled bond motion produces a leading quadratic logical-state dependence of the boundary entropy. We select one state-independent physical recovery by optimizing coherent information of the channel Choi state. A separate semidefinite program maximizes entanglement fidelity over all channels. At fixed coupling the physical recovery attains that optimum to numerical precision, while recovered-entropy subtraction leaves a nonzero variational proto-area response. Total stabilizer Renyi magic is also nonzero for deformations with no boundary response, so it does not characterize the effect. Projecting the exact stabilizer-Renyi quadratic form away from pairwise Pauli tangents defines a leading-order boundary witness. Its global convex minimum is positive for the bond-moving deformation and zero for the six comparisons. For the reference-matter-geometry resource partition, however, pairwise tangents span the full projective tangent space at the product-EPR base, so the witness vanishes for every Hermitian deformation. A two-bound extension retains this degeneracy but exhibits a split-geometry structural residual and a logical-state-dependent cut switch. These results establish a finite-code witness, not a universal magic law or continuum gravitational dynamics.

</details>


### [105] [The Euler-Heisenberg action for a $U(1)\times U(1)$ dyon quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10906)
*D. O. R. Azevedo,T. S. Dias,E. D. Pereira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用Schwinger固有时方法推导了具有U(1)×U(1)规范对称性的dyon量子电动力学（dQED）的单圈有效拉格朗日量，识别了Schwinger对产生极限，并在纯磁背景下线性化运动方程，得到了有效介电常数和磁导率张量、色散关系以及折射率，发现真空表现出双折射现象，所有结果在磁荷为零时退化为标准Euler-Heisenberg结果。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将标准QED推广到包含磁荷的dyon情形，研究dQED在强磁场背景下的量子真空性质，特别是非线性电磁响应和双折射现象。

Method: 采用Schwinger固有时方法推导单圈有效拉格朗日量；通过线性化运动方程分析电磁响应；求解平面波解获得介电常数、磁导率张量、色散关系和折射率。

Result: 得到了dQED的有效拉格朗日量；发现了Schwinger对产生极限；获得了有效介电常数和磁导率张量；折射率涉及两个规范扇区的混合叠加；在对称情形$q_{(1)}=q_{(2)}$下进行了展示；发现真空呈现双折射现象；所有结果在磁荷为零时退化为标准QED的Euler-Heisenberg预言。

Conclusion: dQED在纯磁背景下的量子真空表现为各向异性介质，展现出真空双折射效应，表明磁荷的存在会改变量子真空的光学性质。

Abstract: The one-loop effective Lagrangian of quantum electrodynamics for dyons (dQED) with a $U(1) \times U(1)$ gauge symmetry is derived using the Schwinger proper-time method. We identify the analog of the Schwinger pair-production limit and compute the corresponding nonlinear equations of motion. The electromagnetic response of the model is analyzed by linearizing the equations of motion around a purely magnetic background. From the resulting plane-wave solutions, we obtain the effective permittivity and permeability tensors, along with the associated dispersion relations and refractive indices for parallel and perpendicular polarization modes. The refractive indices involve hybrid superpositions of both gauge sectors, as illustrated in the symmetric case $q_{(1)} = q_{(2)}$. In this background, the model exhibits vacuum birefringence, indicating that the quantum vacuum behaves as an anisotropic medium. All results consistently reduce to the standard Euler-Heisenberg predictions of QED when the magnetic charge vanishes.

</details>


### [106] [Krylov Complexity from Loschmidt Amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10921)
*Debarghya Chakraborty*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了Krylov复杂度与Loschmidt振幅之间的解析联系，将Krylov复杂度表示为依赖于角度变量的Loschmidt振幅的导数，并引入谱传播子来编码复杂度分布。进一步研究了时间与角度变量张成的二维量子几何，证明Krylov复杂度受其体积上限约束，并基于Lanczos系数的增长提出了复杂度动力学的分类方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov复杂度是量子动力学中诊断量子混沌和算子增长的重要工具，而Loschmidt振幅的衰减在特定系统中受经典Lyapunov指数控制。现有研究缺乏将两者直接联系起来的统一理论框架，本文旨在建立这种形式化联系，并探索由此导出的几何解释和分类方案。

Method: 利用Krylov复杂度算子的代数性质，将Krylov复杂度表示为依赖于角度变量φ的Loschmidt振幅对φ的导数；定义谱传播子以编码完整的复杂度分布；研究时间t与φ张成的二维量子几何，证明复杂度受该几何体积的上界约束；通过分析Lanczos系数的增长行为，提出基于微扰强度的复杂度动力学分类方案。

Result: 建立了Krylov复杂度与Loschmidt振幅的精确导数关系；引入了谱传播子概念；证明了复杂度受二维量子几何体积的上界约束；将Krylov复杂度的时间导数用另一种Loschmidt振幅表示；基于Lanczos系数增长提出了复杂度动力学的分类准则；推导出存活振幅时间依赖性与Krylov空间测度之间的普适关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为Krylov复杂度提供了新的解析几何视角，揭示其与Loschmidt振幅的深层联系，并提出了复杂度动力学的分类方案，为理解量子混沌和算子增长提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Krylov complexity is a powerful diagnostic of quantum dynamics, with clear connections to other measures of quantum chaos and operator growth. One such measure is the Loschmidt amplitude, defined as the overlap of initially identical states evolved under two slightly different Hamiltonians. Its decay in certain systems is controlled by the classical Lyapunov exponent. Using the algebraic properties of the Krylov complexity operator, we express Krylov complexity as the derivative of a Loschmidt amplitude whose perturbation is parameterized by an angular variable $φ$. This formulation allows us to define a spectral propagator that encodes the entire complexity distribution, which we characterize for specific types of systems. We study the two-dimensional quantum geometry spanned by time and $φ$ where the original and deformed trajectories reside, demonstrating that Krylov complexity is upper-bounded by its volume. We also express the time derivative of Krylov complexity in terms of a distinct Loschmidt amplitude. Depending on the growth of the Lanczos coefficients, the perturbation term in this amplitude can be truncated. We propose that the strength of this perturbation provides a classification scheme for Krylov complexity dynamics and relate it to the $φ$-derivative of the spectral propagator. Using this analytical framework, we derive general relations between the time-dependence of the survival amplitude and Krylov space measures.

</details>


### [107] [Revisiting wormhole-induced global symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10981)
*Kiyoharu Kawana*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证了量子引力中的虫洞效应对全局对称性的破坏并非必然：在广泛条件下，对α参数的系综平均会被对称点主导，对称性破缺贡献被双指数因子压制，因此U(1) Peccei-Quinn模型中的轴子质量问题不会出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力中普遍认为全局对称性不存在，一个主要机制是引力瞬子/欧几里得虫洞通过α参数产生对称性破缺算子。本文质疑这一结论是否必然成立，即虫洞是否真的明确破坏全局对称性。

Method: 通过对α参数进行系综平均分析，计算不同临界点的贡献权重，特别关注对称点（α=0）与非对称点（α≠0）的相对贡献，比较其指数因子尺度。

Result: 对称点α=0在系综平均中占主导地位，而非对称点α≠0的贡献被双指数因子exp(-e^{2S_ins})强烈压制，其中S_ins为瞬子作用量。标准U(1) Peccei-Quinn模型属于此类情况。

Conclusion: 虫洞效应并不必然破坏全局对称性；在广泛参数条件下对称性可以保持。传统轴子质量问题在虫洞诱导的有效理论中不会出现。该分析适用于一般的U(1) p-形式全局对称性。

Abstract: It is widely believed that global symmetries cannot exist in a consistent theory of quantum gravity. A prominent mechanism underlying this expectation is provided by gravitational instantons or Euclidean wormholes, whose contributions to the Euclidean path integral generates local symmetry-breaking operators in the effective action with couplings parametrized by the $α$-parameters $\overrightarrowα=(α_1^{},α_2^{},\cdots)$. The appearance of these $α$-parameters is often taken as evidence that wormholes explicitly break global symmetries. In this paper, we argue that this conclusion is premature. The fate of the symmetry is determined by the ensemble average over these $α$-parameters. We show that, under broad situations, this average can be sharply dominated by the symmetric point $\overrightarrowα=0$, while contributions from other symmetry-breaking critical points $\overrightarrowα\neq 0$ are typically suppressed by a doubly exponential factor $\exp(-e^{2S_{\rm ins}})$, where $S_{\rm ins}$ is the instanton action. In particular, standard $\mathrm{U}(1)^{}$ Peccei--Quinn models fall into this class, implying that the conventional axion quality problem does not arise in the wormhole-induced effective theory. Our analysis is formulated for general $\mathrm{U}(1)^{}$ $p$-form global symmetries.

</details>


### [108] [A TQFT perspective on satellite constructions and toral decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11108)
*Nikolaos Angelinos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从三维拓扑量子场论（TQFT）的角度研究扭结理论中的卫星构造和拼接构造，将其视为链环补空间态的构建操作。通过沿环面边界粘合流形，TQFT赋予这些构造多线性算子，并与JSJ分解紧密相关。作者以Seifert纤维化链环补、Hopf链补及Whitehead加倍Hopf链补为例，展示了不同JSJ结构导致的纠缠熵模式差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将扭结理论中的经典卫星和拼接构造纳入TQFT框架，建立链环补空间态的代数化构建方案，并揭示其与JSJ分解的内在联系，从而从拓扑量子场论视角理解不同链环补空间态的纠缠结构。

Method: 利用TQFT在带多个环面边界的链环补流形上定义态，将卫星和拼接操作实现为沿环面边界粘合的流形操作，并赋予其作用在环面Hilbert空间上的多线性算子。通过这一框架与JSJ分解对应，将链环补空间态分解为JSJ基本块对应的基本算子网络。

Result: 给出了卫星和拼接构造在TQFT中的显式算子表示，建立了链环补空间态与JSJ分解的对应关系，并通过Seifert纤维化链环补、Hopf链补和Whitehead加倍Hopf链补的纠缠熵计算，展示了不同JSJ拓扑结构如何产生不同的纠缠模式。

Conclusion: TQFT为卫星和拼接构造提供了一个统一的代数框架，并通过JSJ分解自然地揭示了链环补空间态的层次化纠缠结构，不同JSJ拓扑单元对应不同的纠缠熵行为。

Abstract: We consider the classical satellite and splice constructions of knot theory from the perspective of link-complement states in three-dimensional topological quantum field theory (TQFT). These states are prepared by a TQFT on a link-complement manifold, namely a manifold with multiple disjoint torus boundaries, obtained by removing a thickened link from a closed ambient three-manifold. In this setting, satellite and splice constructions are realized by gluing manifolds along torus boundaries, and the TQFT assigns to these building blocks multilinear operators acting on the corresponding torus Hilbert spaces. We give explicit prescriptions for these operators and show how they can be used to build link-complement states. This construction is closely related to the JSJ decomposition of link-complement manifolds. While splicing builds manifolds by gluing along tori, the JSJ decomposition cuts a manifold along essential tori into canonical pieces. From the TQFT point of view, this expresses a link-complement state as a network of elementary operators associated with the JSJ pieces. We illustrate this framework by studying the entanglement entropy of Seifert-fibered link complements, Hopf-chain complements, and complements of Whitehead-doubled Hopf chains, showing how their different JSJ structures lead to distinct entanglement patterns.

</details>


### [109] [Evading Cosmological Strong Coupling in Non-minimally Coupled Vector Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11125)
*Antonio De Felice,Seishi Enomoto,Nagisa Hiroshima,Atsushi Naruko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究Proca理论中非最小曲率耦合导致的标量自由度零传播速度问题，通过引入额外的导数相互作用恢复标量扰动的非退化二次动力学，并在参数空间开区域中实现正定性和稳定的高频传播速度，同时发现非均匀极限行为及稳定的de Sitter不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Proca理论中非最小曲率耦合会引入一个标量自由度，其传播速度恒为零（c_s^2=0），这标志着尺度依赖的强耦合问题。本文旨在解决这一零速度简并问题，恢复标量扰动的正常动力学。

Method: 通过引入额外的导数相互作用，分析标量扰动的二次动力学行为，研究参数空间中动能矩阵的正定性、高频传播速度的实正性，以及A_0→0极限下的非均匀行为。

Result: 额外导数相互作用可解除零速度简并；在参数空间开区域中动能矩阵正定且高频传播速度实正；发现A_0→0时紫外区域出现鬼场，但鬼场出现能标随A_0→0推向无穷；零凝聚分支具有不同的动力学结构；识别出稳定的de Sitter不动点（A_0≠0）及其周围稳定区域。

Conclusion: 该机制有效消除了c_s^2=0模式对应的强耦合特征，但鬼场问题是否超出EFT截断需独立确定；稳定的de Sitter解为宇宙学应用提供可能。

Abstract: Recent analyses of Proca theories with non-minimal curvature couplings have uncovered an additional scalar degree of freedom with an identically vanishing propagation speed, $c_s^2=0$, signaling a scale-dependent strong-coupling problem. In this {\it Letter}, we show that an additional derivative interaction can lift this zero-speed degeneracy and restore a non-degenerate quadratic dynamics for the scalar perturbations. In an open region of parameter space, the scalar kinetic matrix is positive definite and the high-frequency propagation speeds are real and positive. At quadratic order, this removes the specific signature of strong coupling associated with the $c_s^2=0$ mode. We also uncover a non-uniform limit as the temporal vector condensate approaches zero: at fixed nonzero $A_0$, the formal extreme-ultraviolet regime develops a ghost, while the kinetic structure of the exactly vanishing-condensate branch is different. The momentum scale at which this ghost appears is pushed toward increasingly high values as $A_0\to0$. Whether the ghost scale ultimately lies above the EFT cutoff depends on an independent determination of the EFT cutoff. Finally, we identify a stable de Sitter fixed point with $A_0\neq0$, surrounded by a finite region in which the scalar no-ghost and high-frequency stability conditions remain satisfied.

</details>


### [110] [Bethe Ansatz without Nesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11617)
*Gleb Arutyunov,Hrachya Babujian,Minghao Gao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对有理 $\mathfrak{gl}_\ell$ 自旋链（矢量表示）的非嵌套Bethe ansatz描述。从量子谱曲线和分离变量框架出发，推导出仅涉及动量承载Bethe根的封闭Bethe方程组。核心结果是识别了与基本转移矩阵相关的递归层次结构，该结构通过一个秩为 $\ell$ 的普适方程 $\mathcal{R}_{\ell}=0$ 闭合，消除了所有辅助Bethe根，使得本征态的全部谱数据仅由第一个Baxter多项式 $Q_1(u)$ 编码。文章还给出了所有基本转移矩阵特征值在动量承载根下的显式表达式，并研究了准经典（Gaudin）极限，以 $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ 为例展示了其与标量三阶 $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ oper 的自然联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的嵌套Bethe ansatz在处理高秩自旋链时引入了大量辅助Bethe根，使得方程结构复杂且冗余。本文旨在发展一种非嵌套的描述方式，消除辅助根，简化方程结构，从而更紧凑地表征有理 $\mathfrak{gl}_\ell$ 自旋链的谱数据。

Method: 从量子谱曲线和分离变量（SoV）框架出发，推导仅含动量承载Bethe根的封闭Bethe方程。通过研究基本转移矩阵的递归层次结构，利用低阶转移矩阵的正则性条件生成该层次，并以普适方程 $\mathcal{R}_{\ell}=0$ 闭合。利用该结构，将全部谱数据编码在第一个Baxter多项式 $Q_1(u)$ 中，并给出所有基本转移矩阵特征值的显式表达式。最后，通过准经典极限分析，将非嵌套Bethe方程简化为Gaudin方程，并关联到标量高阶oper。

Result: 成功构建了有理 $\mathfrak{gl}_\ell$ 自旋链的非嵌套Bethe ansatz描述。具体结果包括：1）推导出仅含动量承载根的封闭Bethe方程组；2）识别了由正则性条件生成的递归层次结构，并以 $\mathcal{R}_{\ell}=0$ 闭合；3）证明了本征态的全部谱数据仅由 $Q_1(u)$ 编码；4）给出了所有基本转移矩阵特征值在动量承载根下的显式表达式；5）在准经典极限下，以 $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ 为例得到了无极点形式的Gaudin方程，自然定义了标量三阶 $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ oper。

Conclusion: 本文提供的非嵌套Bethe ansatz框架显著简化了高秩自旋链的谱问题，消除了传统嵌套方案中的辅助根冗余，揭示了量子谱曲线、转移矩阵融合关系与截断 $Q$-系统之间的直接联系。该工作为有理 $\mathfrak{gl}_\ell$ 自旋链的谱理论提供了一种紧凑且自洽的描述，并在准经典极限下与高阶oper理论建立了自然关联。

Abstract: We develop a non-nested Bethe ansatz description of rational $\mathfrak{gl}_\ell$ spin chains in the vector representation. Starting from the quantum spectral curve and the separation-of-variables framework, we derive closed systems of Bethe equations involving only the momentum-carrying Bethe roots. The construction is worked out explicitly for the $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ and $\mathfrak{gl}_4$ spin chains and then generalized to arbitrary rank. A central result of this work is the identification of a recursive hierarchy associated with the fundamental transfer matrices. The hierarchy is generated by regularity conditions of the lower transfer matrices and closes through a universal rank-$\ell$ equation $\mathcal{R}_{\ell}=0$. This equation replaces the final level of the conventional nested Bethe ansatz and eliminates all auxiliary Bethe roots. Consequently, the complete spectral data of an eigenstate are encoded solely in the first Baxter polynomial $Q_{1}(u)$. We further obtain explicit expressions for the eigenvalues of all fundamental transfer matrices in terms of the momentum-carrying roots alone. The resulting formulation provides a compact characterization of the spectrum of rational $\mathfrak{gl}_\ell$ spin chains and reveals a direct connection between the quantum spectral curve, transfer-matrix fusion relations, and a truncated $Q$-system underlying the non-nested description. Finally, we investigate the quasi-classical (Gaudin) limit of the non-nested Bethe equations. For the $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ spin chain, we show that the leading non-trivial contribution gives rise to Gaudin equations whose pole-free form naturally defines a scalar third-order $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ oper.

</details>


### [111] [Conjugate Boundary Conditions, Kaluza-Klein Fermions, and an Extended Seesaw Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11150)
*Yugo Abe,Yuki Adachi,Yukihiro Fujimoto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在紧致五维理论（M⁴⊗S¹/Z₂）中通过共轭边界条件（CBC）实现马约拉纳费米子的方案。研究发现，虽然零模是马约拉纳费米子，但非零KK模在自由理论中可组合成狄拉克旋量，这源于KK模扇区存在偶然U(1)对称性。同时探讨了CBC与手征轨道投影兼容的相互作用，并构建了一种扩展跷跷板机制，其中新相互作用导致轻子数破坏，而马约拉纳质量项不破坏轻子数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究共轭边界条件（CBC）在紧致五维理论中实现马约拉纳费米子的可行性，并厘清零模与非零KK模的旋量结构差异，同时探索与跷跷板机制相关的潜在应用。

Method: 通过直接计算分析CBC下零模和非零KK模的费米子场结构；利用轴U(1)变换的相容性对KK模进行对角化；引入与手征轨道投影兼容的体相互作用，构建扩展跷跷板模型。

Result: 零模为马约拉纳费米子，而非零KK模可组合成狄拉克旋量，因KK模扇区存在偶然U(1)对称性；CBC与手征轨道投影允许非平庸体相互作用；构建的扩展跷跷板模型中，新相互作用导致轻子数破坏，而马约拉纳质量项不破坏轻子数。

Conclusion: CBC在紧致五维理论中能实现马约拉纳费米子零模，但KK模具有更丰富的旋量结构；CBC与手征投影的兼容性允许新型相互作用，为构建具有不同轻子数破坏特性的扩展跷跷板机制提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we discuss the conjugate boundary condition (CBC), which has recently been studied as a way of realizing a Majorana fermion within a compactified five-dimensional theory ($\mathcal M^4\otimes S^1/Z_2$). The Majorana fermion which plays a crucial role in the seesaw scenario arises as a zero mode (lowest mode) by imposing the CBC. Although each nonzero Kaluza-Klein (KK) mode is also naively expected to be described by two Majorana fermions, we show by direct calculation that they can be combined into a Dirac spinor in the free theory or up to the level of the quadratic term in the Lagrangian. This difference comes from the fact that an accidental U(1) symmetry exists in the KK mode sector, though such a symmetry does not appear in the zero mode sector. We also point out that the compatibility between the CBC and the axial U(1) transformation plays a crucial role in the diagonalization of the KK mode.
  We also investigate interactions compatible with both the CBC and the chiral orbifold projection. We find that a nontrivial bulk interaction between a CBC fermion and a chiral-orbifold fermion is allowed. As an application, we construct an extended seesaw scenario by utilizing both boundary conditions and such nontrivial bulk interactions. The resulting seesaw scenario has a different property in contrast with the conventional extended seesaw scenario; namely, the above new non-trivial interactions cause lepton number violation (LNV), while the Majorana mass term in our model does not violate lepton number.

</details>


### [112] [Belinfante Symmetrization from Metric-Affine Conservation Laws: Hypermomentum as the Improvement Term -- The Cases of QED and QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11322)
*Damianos Iosifidis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从度规-仿射守恒律出发，通过平直时空场论的仿射提升，推导出Belinfante-Rosenfeld对称化程序。将独立仿射联络最小耦合后，对联络变分定义超动量流。取平直时空极限，得到Belinfante-Rosenfeld关系，其中超动量流的散度重现Belinfante改进项。详细研究了具有该性质的耦合形式及其物理意义，并以QED和QCD为例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供Belinfante-Rosenfeld对称化程序的几何化推导，将其与度规-仿射引力中的超动量概念建立直接联系，从而从更基本的几何原理理解能量-动量张量的对称化过程。

Method: 采用仿射提升方法，将平直时空场论耦合到独立仿射联络，通过对联络变分定义超动量流，取平直时空极限恢复Belinfante-Rosenfeld关系。

Result: 成功从度规-仿射守恒律推导出Belinfante-Rosenfeld对称化程序，超动量流的散度精确对应Belinfante改进项，并在QED和QCD中验证了该构造。

Conclusion: Belinfante-Rosenfeld对称化可以理解为度规-仿射框架下平直时空极限的自然结果，为该程序提供了更深刻的几何基础。

Abstract: We derive the Belinfante--Rosenfeld symmetrization procedure from the metric-affine conservation law by means of an affine lift of flat-spacetime field theories. Following minimal coupling to an independent affine connection, variation of the action with respect to the connection defines the hypermomentum current. Taking the flat-spacetime limit of the resulting conservation law, we recover the Belinfante--Rosenfeld relation, with the divergence of the hypermomentum current reproducing the Belinfante improvement term. We thoroughly study the form of the couplings with this property and their physical significance. The construction is illustrated for Quantum Electrodynamics and Quantum Chromodynamics.

</details>


### [113] [Large $N$ conformal bootstrap and chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11332)
*J. A. Gracey*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用大N临界点形式主义，研究了超对称性和手征对称性对若干相关场论重正化群函数中多重zeta值位置的影响，并确定了O(N) Wess-Zumino模型玻色子扇区的物质场反常维度在O(1/N^3)阶上无多重zeta值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨超对称性和手征对称性如何影响场论中多重zeta值的出现，并验证特定模型中是否存在此类超越结构。

Method: 采用大N临界点形式主义，分析O(N) Wess-Zumino模型玻色子扇区等场论的重正化群函数。

Result: 确定了O(N) Wess-Zumino模型玻色子扇区的物质场反常维度在O(1/N^3)阶上不包含多重zeta值。

Conclusion: 超对称性和手征对称性对场论中多重zeta值的出现有显著影响，特定模型在给定阶数下可避免此类超越结构。

Abstract: We use the large $N$ critical point formalism to examine the interplay of supersymmetry and chiral symmetry on the location or otherwise of multiple zetas in the renormalization group functions of several related field theories. In particular we determine that the anomalous dimension of the matter field of the Lagrangian corresponding to the bosonic sector of the $O(N)$ Wess-Zumino is free of multiple zetas to $O(1/N^3)$.

</details>


### [114] [Brouwer Degree of Thermodynamic Multicritical Points in Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11394)
*Bidyut Hazarika,Prabwal Phukon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黑洞热容的拓扑框架，通过构造二维热力学向量场，利用Brouwer度刻画临界点的局部拓扑性质，证明了AdS黑洞系统中存在全局守恒的拓扑荷，并揭示了拓扑转变由携带相反Brouwer度的拓扑中性缺陷对产生或湮灭所驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑洞热力学研究主要集中在单个临界点的分析，缺乏对热力学多临界点之间拓扑关系及其演化规律的统一理解框架。

Method: 基于黑洞热容构造二维热力学向量场，将临界点视为向量场的孤立零点，利用Brouwer度表征每个临界点的局部拓扑性质，并计算系统总拓扑荷。

Result: 每个AdS黑洞系统均存在一个全局守恒的拓扑荷；热力学参数变化时，尽管临界点数量改变，总拓扑荷保持不变；拓扑转变源于携带相反Brouwer度的拓扑中性缺陷对的产生或湮灭。

Conclusion: 该拓扑框架为理解黑洞热力学多临界点的涌现、演化和分类提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we propose a novel topological framework based on the heat capacity of black holes to investigate the topology of thermodynamic multicritical points. We construct a two-dimensional thermodynamic vector field whose isolated zeros correspond to the critical points of the system. The local topology of each critical point is characterized by its Brouwer degree, which serves as a locally conserved topological quantity. Applying this formalism to several AdS black hole solutions, we demonstrate that each system possesses a globally conserved topological charge. Although the number of thermodynamic critical points changes as the thermodynamic parameters are varied, the total topological charge remains invariant throughout the evolution. We show that these topological transitions are governed by the creation or annihilation of topologically neutral defect pairs carrying opposite Brouwer degrees. Our results provide a unified topological framework for understanding the emergence, evolution, and classification of thermodynamic multicritical points in black hole systems.

</details>


### [115] [Superconformal mechanics from N-extended Euler-Calogero-Moser and Calogero models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11468)
*S. Krivonos,A. Sutulin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了两粒子版本的N扩展Euler-Calogero-Moser和Calogero模型。由于平移不变性，质心可解耦，留下特定超共形力学。Euler-Calogero-Moser模型的两粒子情形中超对称性可从Osp(N|2)意外扩展至SU(1,1|N)，而Calogero模型则保持Osp(N|2)超共形力学。系统中存在更多费米子可解决相关问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两粒子变体的N扩展Euler-Calogero-Moser和Calogero模型，探索其超共形对称性结构，并解决该路径中的已知问题。

Method: 利用平移不变性解耦质心及相应费米子，将额外的玻色变量（自旋变量）纳入超荷与哈密顿量中，分析两粒子情形下的超对称扩展结构。

Result: Euler-Calogero-Moser模型的两粒子情形中超对称性从Osp(N|2)扩展至SU(1,1|N)；Calogero模型仅保持Osp(N|2)超共形对称性。系统中引入更多费米子可规避相关问题。

Conclusion: 两粒子变体可导出不同的超共形力学结构，增加费米子数量是解决该路径中已知问题的有效方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we considered two-particle variants of the N-extended Euler-Calogero-Moser and Calogero models. Due the translation invariance, the center of mass can be decoupled (together with the corresponding fermions), leaving us with specific superconformal mechanics. Additional bosonic variables (spin variables) can easily be incorporated into the supercharges and the Hamiltonian. In the case of the Euler-Calogero-Moser model, the supersymmetry in the two-particle cases admits an unexpected extension from Osp(N|2) to SU(1,1|N) superconformal symmetry. The case of the Calogero model leads to purely Osp(N|2) superconformal mechanics. The way to get rid of the well-known problems along this path is to have a higher number of fermions present in the system.

</details>


### [116] [Multi-rotating black holes with non-aligned angular momenta in 5D Kaluza-Klein theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11609)
*Shinya Tomizawa,Hideo Furugori,Jun-ichi Sakamoto,Ryotaku Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了4D爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量理论中多旋转黑洞的精确解，通过5D卡鲁扎-克莱因理论维度约化得到。该解描述了携带电荷和磁荷的多中心旋转黑洞构型，每个黑洞具有非对齐的角动量，推广了先前对齐角动量的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将之前对齐角动量的多旋转黑洞解推广到更一般的非对齐角动量情形，以构建更普适的多旋转黑洞精确解。

Method: 通过5D卡鲁扎-克莱因理论的维度约化，得到4D爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量理论的精确解。

Result: 获得了多中心旋转黑洞构型的精确解，每个黑洞携带电量和磁荷且角动量非对齐。该解包含Majumdar-Papapetrou解、Teo和Wan的多中心旋转黑洞解以及先前不等电荷解作为特例。当每个黑洞的自旋角动量低于某上界时，时空在视界内外均无曲率奇点和闭合类时曲线。

Conclusion: 成功构建了非对齐角动量的多旋转黑洞精确解，且在一定条件下该解是物理上良定义的（无奇点、无闭合类时曲线）。

Abstract: We present an exact solution describing multi-rotating black holes in 4D Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory, which can be obtained from 5D Kaluza--Klein theory via dimensional reduction. The solution represents a multi-centered configuration of rotating black holes carrying both electric and magnetic charges, with each black hole possessing a non-aligned angular momentum. This work generalizes our previous solution for black holes with aligned angular momenta to the more general case of non-aligned angular momenta. It includes, as special cases, the Majumdar--Papapetrou solution, the recent multi-centered rotating black hole solutions of Teo and Wan, and our previous solution with unequal electric and magnetic charges. The resulting spacetimes are free of curvature singularities and closed timelike curves, both on and outside the horizons, provided that the magnitude of the spin angular momentum of each black hole remains below a certain upper bound.

</details>


### [117] [Holographic Timelike Entanglement and Subregion Complexity in Localized AdS3*S3*T4 Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11641)
*Jitendra Pal,Yu Shi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了局域化黑洞（黑极解）中的类时纠缠熵和类时子区域复杂度，发现其与BTZ解不同，对内部球面有非平凡依赖，且类时复杂度给出实有限重整化体积。通过局域化类时方案，揭示了大r区主导角依赖消失、时间族非单调等特性，表明类时可观测可探测BTZ和大r描述中不存在的局域化几何效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索局域化黑洞（黑极解）的类时洛伦兹可观测量的性质，揭示BTZ解无法捕捉的局域化几何效应。

Method: 基于AdS3*S3*T4背景下的黑极解，构造类时纠缠熵和类时子区域复杂度。采用局域化类时方案，在角标$θ_0$处构建分支轮廓并提升到物理内角$θ$，分析大r渐近行为和精确几何中的时间族结构。

Result: 类时纠缠熵产生复提升面积，类时复杂度给出实有限重整化体积。大r区角依赖消失，恢复短间隔行为。精确黑极几何中时间族非单调，需固定边界间隔选择。随间隔增大，分支向内移动并感知cap-视界过渡区域。

Conclusion: 类时洛伦兹可观测能够探测BTZ和大r主导描述中不存在的局域化几何效应，为研究黑洞微观结构提供了新探针。

Abstract: We study timelike entanglement entropy and timelike subregion complexity in localized black holes with asymptotic AdS3*S3*T4 geometry, focusing on the black-pole solution. Unlike the BTZ solution, the black pole exhibits a nontrivial dependence on the internal sphere through the functions $K_y(r,θ)$ and $G(r,θ)$. Both observables are constructed from spacelike and timelike Lorentzian branches, but they probe the geometry in different ways: timelike entanglement yields a complex lifted area, while timelike complexity gives a real, finite renormalized volume. We employ a localized timelike prescription in which the branch profile is built at an angular label $θ_0$ and subsequently lifted over the physical internal angle $θ$. In the large-$r$ regime, the leading angular dependence drops out, recovering the expected short-interval behaviour. In the exact black-pole geometry, the temporal families become non-monotonic, making a fixed-boundary-interval selection essential. As the boundary interval increases, the selected branches move inward and become sensitive to the localized cap-horizon transition region. These results demonstrate that timelike Lorentzian observables probe localized-geometry effects that are absent in BTZ and in the leading large-$r$ description.

</details>


### [118] [Notes on the bootstrap of four-point conformal integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11645)
*Xuhang Jiang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自举工作流，利用前奇点、单值多重对数多项式和边界数据（通过区域展开获得）研究位置空间中的四点共形积分，成功计算了三环中14/15个不等价积分基（最后一个通过Gram恒等式固定），并将方法推广至四环情况，通过分解具有多个前奇点的积分解决了其他方法难以处理的积分问题，同时提供了适用于当前AI模型的技能文件包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究位置空间中一般的四点共形积分，特别是处理非平面f-图扇区生成的高阶共形积分，解决四环及更高阶次中现有方法难以计算或无法计算的积分问题。

Method: 采用自举工作流，结合前奇点分析、单值多重对数多项式ansätze以及区域展开的边界数据；对三环情况使用HyperlogProcedures直接计算，辅以Gram恒等式；对四环情况通过将具有多个前奇点的积分分解为具有更简单割结构的子积分来处理。

Result: 三环中14个不等价积分基可通过HyperlogProcedures直接计算，最后一个由Gram恒等式确定；四环中一些原本难以计算或无法通过其他方法计算的积分被成功求解。

Conclusion: 该自举工作流对三环和四环四点共形积分均有效，通过分解策略使多前奇点积分变得可处理，同时提供了适合AI模型使用的技能文件包，为更高阶共形积分的计算提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We set up a bootstrap workflow to study four-point conformal integrals in position space, using leading singularities, single-valued multiple polylogarithmic ansätze and boundary data from expansion by regions. These four-point conformal integrals are general in the sense that they are generated by the four-point projections of all possible $f$-graphs, including all non-planar $f$-graph sectors. For three-loop cases, fourteen of the fifteen inequivalent integrand basis can be directly calculated by \texttt{HyperlogProcedures} and the last one is fixed by Gram identity. Then we concentrate on how far the bootstrap workflow can go for four-loop cases, though it works for three-loop cases as well. We show that integrals with several leading singularities can be made tractable by decomposing them into pieces with simpler cut structure. Some four-loop integrals which can not be calculated or very hard to be calculated by other methods for now are obtained in this way. We also provide a package with skill files which is suitable to be read and used by current AI models.

</details>


### [119] [Modular structures in the DSSYK partition function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11828)
*Matteo Beccaria,Eleonora Alfinito*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了双标度SYK模型（DSSYK）在低温下盘配分函数的展开，发现其可由拟模艾森斯坦级数环组织，并通过模S-对偶性推导出半经典低温展开，包含微扰和非微扰部分；非微扰修正可重求和为紧致形式，且整体结构来自一个精确微分方程；最终证明非微扰部分在所有λ阶上精确支持与已知双局域-刘维尔鞍点作用量相同的指数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解DSSYK模型在低温极限下的精确行为，特别是非微扰修正的结构及其与拟模形式和模对偶性的关系，并揭示其与引力对偶中鞍点作用量的联系。

Method: 利用Bessel函数精确表示盘配分函数Z(β)，在低温（大β）展开中识别拟模艾森斯坦级数E₂, E₄, E₆及其微分恒等式；应用模S-对偶性推导半经典（小λ）展开；通过微分方程将模导数与温度导数耦合，统一描述整个结构。

Result: 低温展开可组织为拟模艾森斯坦级数形式；半经典展开分为微扰塔和非微扰部分（由q̃=e^{-4π²/λ}控制），每个q̃阶的非微扰修正可闭式求至λ二阶；整个结构满足一个精确微分方程；非微扰部分的指数在所有λ阶上精确匹配DSSYK Schwarzschian极限中双局域-刘维尔鞍点的在壳作用量。

Conclusion: DSSYK模型的低温非微扰修正具有深刻的模形式和可积结构，且与引力对偶中的鞍点有明确对应，支持其体视界起源。

Abstract: We study the low-temperature expansion of the disk partition function $Z(β)$ of the double-scaled SYK model (DSSYK) at fixed coupling $λ=2p^{2}/N$, where $N$ is the number of Majorana fermions and $p$ is the number of fermions in each interaction term, both taken to infinity. We show that the exact Bessel-function representation of $Z(β)$, expanded at large argument (corresponding to low temperature), can be organized in terms of the classical ring of quasi-modular Eisenstein series $E_{2},E_{4},E_{6}$ and their differential identities. Exploiting the modular $S$-duality properties of this ring, we derive the semiclassical (small $λ$) low-temperature expansion of $Z(β)$, splitting it into a perturbative tower and a non-perturbative sector controlled by $\widetilde q=e^{-4π^{2}/λ}$. At each order in $\widetilde q$, we determine the non-perturbative correction in closed form up to second order in $λ$; the resulting series resums into a compact expression in the same Eisenstein series, extending previous semiclassical results beyond their strict $β\to\infty$ limit. We further show that this entire structure follows from a single, exact differential equation coupling a modular derivative to derivatives with respect to temperature. Finally, we prove that the non-perturbative sector of $Z(β)$ is exactly supported, to all orders in $λ$, on the same exponents as the on-shell actions of known bilocal-Liouville saddles of the DSSYK Schwarzian limit, pointing to a well-defined bulk origin for these non-perturbative corrections.

</details>


### [120] [Effective dynamics and quantum information in de Sitter wedge holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11848)
*Sabyasachi Maulik,Soumen Pari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究dS楔形全息对偶，提出(d+1)维AdS体时空与两个d维dS端面边界膜构成的系统，全息对偶于(d-1)维球面上的非幺正CFT，并计算配分函数、全息纠缠熵、质量谱，验证纠缠熵第一定律，最后利用岛规则研究Page曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索de Sitter背景下的全息对偶性，特别是利用楔形全息（wedge holography）的思想构建dS时空的低维全息描述，以理解dS时空的量子引力性质。

Method: 采用楔形全息框架，考虑(d+1)维AdS体时空被两个具有d维dS几何的端面边界膜（end-of-the-world branes）所截断，通过计算配分函数、全息纠缠熵、质量谱等来验证该对偶关系。

Result: 支持了dS楔形全息对偶的存在性，发现对偶CFT是非幺正的；验证了纠缠熵第一定律；在简化模型中利用岛规则研究了Page曲线。

Conclusion: dS楔形全息提供了一个将dS时空全息对偶到CFT的有效框架，尽管该CFT是非幺正的，但该框架可用于研究dS时空中的量子纠缠和信息悖论等问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study codimension-two holography in a de Sitter (dS) wedge setup, based on the idea of wedge holography. We consider a $d+1$-dimensional Anti-de Sitter (AdS) bulk spacetime bounded by two end-of-the-world branes with $d$-dimensional de Sitter geometry. We propose that this configuration is holographically dual to a conformal field theory (CFT) living on a $d-1$-dimensional sphere. Our computations of the partition function and holographic entanglement entropy support this duality and indicate that the dual CFT is non-unitary. We also analyze the mass spectrum in dS wedge holography. We verify the first law of entanglement entropy within this framework. Finally, we make use of the island prescription to study the Page curve in a simplified model within our dS wedge holography framework.

</details>
